## Mandatory role coverage matrix

`ADMIN` in the requested reporting matrix maps to the source role `SYSTEM_ADMIN`; source role names are not rewritten in requests or authorization assertions.

| Function | ADMIN / SYSTEM_ADMIN | MOTHER | FAMILY | EXPERT |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| View own profile | Allowed (SYSTEM_ADMIN) | Allowed | Allowed | Allowed |
| View another user's private profile | Denied except a separately implemented governance projection | Denied | Denied | Denied |
| View admin audit | Allowed (SYSTEM_ADMIN) | Denied | Denied | Denied |
| Manage consent | Policy/admin-only review where implemented | Owner grant/revoke | Only granted/shared scope | Only explicit consent/scope |
| Expert trust review | Allowed only on SYSTEM_ADMIN review endpoints | Denied | Denied | Own submission/status only |
| Access maternal private data | Only an implemented governance projection | Owner | Granted care-group scope | Consent/shared scope only |

## Mock data

Use the `qa_*` namespace documented in the Postman guide and existing `DevDataSeeder` fixtures only after database confirmation. Passwords remain local secret variables and are never written here.

## Environment boundary

- Runtime profile detected by name: `supabase`.
- The configured Supabase hostname did not include an explicit test/dev/qa/staging marker. No remote database connection or mutation was performed by this generator.
- Backend direct base URL: `http://localhost:8080`; Web: `http://localhost:5173`; AI triage default local service: `http://127.0.0.1:8001`; Android emulator backend alias: `http://10.0.2.2:8080`.
- Required environment variable names are listed in the Postman guide; all values are masked/omitted.

## Register exclusion

Register exists in Report 3 but is excluded from the current MF-01 execution scope because existing Supabase/mock seed accounts are used. `AuthController.register`, mobile `register_screen.dart`, web `ExpertRegisterPage.tsx`, and federated registration UI are not represented as MF-01 test cases.

## Requirement/implementation gaps and scope review

| Requirement or function | Finding |
| --- | --- |
| MF-02 requested generic medication/vitamin/healthcare reminder variants | Implementation uses typed reminder/reminder-schedule APIs; verify exact enum values from DTO/entity before execution. |
| MF-07 ambulance dispatch and guaranteed-arrival behavior | Explicitly not implemented/product claim prohibited; validate absence of such claim. |
| MF-10 certified fall detector/emergency dispatch | Explicitly not implemented/product claim prohibited; phone-sensor safety support only. |
| Report 3 UC-112–UC-115 connected health device/platform | No active Release-1 wearable/connected-medical-device interface found; Report 1 marks connected device scope deferred. |
| Report 3 UC-109–UC-111 Expense Planner | Implementation exists, but it is outside the user's final 10-tab Integration Test scope. |
| Report 3 paid consultation/direct chat/payment flows | The requested MF-05 baseline defers/removes the paid consultation/payment journey. Only current implementation artifacts still discovered in source (for example consultation-request UI or missed-call reconciliation) are retained as source-derived review coverage and require product-scope confirmation before execution. |

## Evidence checklist

- Postman method, URL, non-secret headers, source-derived JSON body, response status and response body.
- Relevant Supabase/disposable PostgreSQL row filtered by the synthetic resource ID.
- UI loading, empty, validation, forbidden and success state when a client screen is involved.
- Audit event for sensitive/admin/consent operations.
- SMTP test mailbox, Firebase/FCM emulator, Gemini/AI stub, TrackAsia mock, Cloudinary/R2 test bucket, CompreFace sandbox or device-permission evidence when applicable.
- Evidence must redact passwords, complete tokens, private keys, service keys and identifiable health data.
